## Hand sabotage and earlier runs

This branch was first built as a longer series, never published, and rebuilt
for review as three code commits and this record (ADR 003: one record per
domain and day). It was rebuilt once more under its current name, with
the same code, to take the host project's names out of comments and test
fixtures; the generated run above is that build's, and it repeats the
previous build's generated run of 2026-11-26 on ecd92ef: 248 mutants run,
239 killed, 2 survived (the two `.anchor/sabotage/survivors.txt` holds as
equivalent: `Lint.put_module_analyses/4` with_passthrough and
`StructGetterConvention.collect_alias/2`'s multi-alias force_condition), 7
invalid, 0 no_tests. The first build's six generated runs are not repeated here;
what each measured, and what killed each survivor it left, is kept below, so
no measured result is dropped. The short SHAs below name commits of that first
build; the code they describe is the code of this branch.

- **Domain:** framework_test_mocks_manager
- **Branch:** athena/DND-8063-framework-tests-mock-manager
- **Date:** 2026-11-26
- **Code under test:** `Anchor.Domain.Checks.FrameworkTestMocksManager`, the
  `Anchor.Check.FrameworkTestMocksManager` shell, the type's `mock_patterns`
  refusal in `Anchor.Domain.RuleSchema`, and Anchor's own `.anchor.yml` Rules 4
  and 4b over `test/mix/tasks/*_test.exs`
- **Tests:** `test/anchor/domain/checks/framework_test_mocks_manager_test.exs`,
  `framework_test_mocks_manager_attribute_names_test.exs`,
  `test/anchor/check/framework_test_mocks_manager_test.exs`,
  `test/anchor/e2e/checks_e2e_test.exs` (F1, F2),
  `test/anchor/domain/rule_checks_nothing_test.exs`
  (`table(:framework_test_mocks_manager)`), and `mix credo --strict` for the
  dogfood rows
- **Suite run:** from the worktree root, `mix test` of the five test files
  above for S4-S13, and `mix credo --strict` for S1-S3 (Elixir 1.18.4, OTP 27)
- **Base:** bfc79a8 (this ticket's first commit)

Each mutation was applied to one file, the named run made, the file restored
with `git checkout`. Failure text is verbatim.

S1-S3 prove the rule is not vacuous on Anchor's own tree: without them the
green `mix credo --strict` would read the same as a rule that selects nothing.

| # | Mutation | Run | Failed | Failure |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | `test/mix/tasks/anchor_baseline_test.exs` drops `setup :verify_on_exit!` | credo, exit 6 | Rule 4 | `Framework test expects on Anchor.ConfigLoaderMock but never verifies its` |
| S2 | `.anchor.yml` Rule 4 `mock_patterns` becomes `Anchor.Managers.*Mock` (Anchor has no Manager double) | credo, exit 2 | Rule 4, nine files | `Framework test sets no expectation on a manager double (a module matching Anchor.Managers.*Mock); its expectations name only` |
| S3 | the same task test gains `defp direct, do: Anchor.Managers.AstBaseline.run([], [])` | credo, exit 2 | Rule 4b | `Module has forbidden direct dependency on Anchor.Managers.AstBaseline.` |
| S4 | verification never required (`%{verified?: :never}`) | mix test, exit 2 | N4, N9, every attribute-name row | `code:  assert detect(source) == ["verify_on_exit!"]` |
| S5 | `stub` counts as `expect` | mix test, exit 2 | N2 | `N2: a stub is not an expectation` `code:  assert [%{trigger: "expect"}] = detect(source)` `129 tests, 1 failure` |
| S6 | aliases not resolved | mix test, exit 2 | P1, P3, N8, e2e F1, F2 | `its expectations name only SearchMock, which no mock_patterns entry matches` `129 tests, 5 failures` |
| S7 | any double counts whatever `mock_patterns` says | mix test, exit 2 | N3, N11 | `code:  assert [violation] = detect(source)` `129 tests, 2 failures` |
| S8 | the file's own `expect` definition ignored | mix test, exit 2 | N6 | `N6: a bare expect is not Mox's when the file defines its own expect` `129 tests, 1 failure` |
| S9 | an attribute's name walked as code | mix test, exit 2 | `@verify_on_exit!`, `@verify!`, `@setup` rows | `code:  assert detect(source) == ["verify_on_exit!"]` `129 tests, 3 failures` |
| S10 | an unresolved double accepted as a manager double | mix test, exit 2 | N5 | `N5: a double that is not a module name fails closed and is named` `129 tests, 1 failure` |
| S11 | any qualifier accepted for `expect` | mix test, exit 2 | N7, N8 | `N8: an alias of another module named Mox does not verify or expect as Mox` `129 tests, 2 failures` |
| S12 | a match-all `mock_patterns` entry accepted | mix test, exit 2 | `table(:framework_test_mocks_manager)` | `framework_test_mocks_manager: every rule that checks nothing is refused, and the positive rows load` `129 tests, 1 failure` |
| S13 | the piped form not read | mix test, exit 2 | P3, N5 | `P3: a piped expect, and a chain of them, name the pipe's head` `129 tests, 2 failures` |

as still lacking a red (`test/anchor_test.exs` "returns list of checks", and
`rule_checks_nothing_test.exs` "exactly these types carry a relation").

|---|---|---|---|---|

| S2 | as above | credo, exit 2 | Rule 4, nine files | `Framework test sets no expectation on a manager double (a module matching Anchor.Managers.*Mock); its expectations name only ...` (once per task test) |
| S3 | as above | credo, exit 2 | Rule 4b | `Module has forbidden direct dependency on Anchor.Managers.AstBaseline. Fix: do not reference Anchor.Managers.* from a module this rule selects; ...` |
| S14 | `lib/anchor.ex`: `Anchor.Check.FrameworkTestMocksManager` left out of `checks/0` | `mix test test/anchor_test.exs`, exit 2 | "returns list of checks", "checks/0 matches every check module that actually exists on disk" | `code:  assert length(checks) == 16` `left:  15` `right: 16` `3 tests, 2 failures` |

### Hand sabotage, third run (on 9b77034, the gate's findings)

The gate on 9b77034 found two shared per-type tables without a row for the
new type; the rows were added and each was red by hand (Elixir 1.18.4, OTP 27):

| # | Mutation | Run | Failed | Failure |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S16 | `fix_shape_invariant_test.exs`: the type's row gives `mock_patterns: ["*"]` (a refused rule) | `mix test test/anchor/domain/fix_shape_invariant_test.exs`, exit 2 | "framework_test_mocks_manager: its canonical violation ends with exactly one Fix:, its rule's shape" | `** (FunctionClauseError) no function clause matching in Anchor.Domain.RuleShapes.for/1` `17 tests, 1 failure` |
| S17 | `rule_shapes_test.exs`: the type built with no `mock_patterns` | `mix test test/anchor/domain/rule_shapes_test.exs`, exit 2 | "for/1 every rule type in RuleSchema.rule_types/0 has a non-empty shape" | `** (FunctionClauseError) no function clause matching in Anchor.Domain.RuleShapes.for/1` `7 tests, 1 failure` |

The generated runs above left survivors; each is answered by a test or a
removal in the commit after the run that found it:

- run 1 (bfc79a8): `definition_name/1` narrowed clauses and
  `elixir_module?/1` returning its input: N6b (guarded, defaulted, zero-arity
  and unquoted definitions; the local check became arity-aware) and N12 (an
  Erlang atom as the double).
- run 2 (55e71da): `call_arity/2` (N14), `mock_node/2` (N13, a chain on a
  double no entry names, whose exact message holds no unresolved clause),
  `others/1` and `unresolved/1` (N1 now asserts the whole message),
  `setup_verifies?/2` returning its list (N15), `refuse_all_mocks/1` and
  `validate/2` (rows in `rule_schema_test.exs`, the file a run of RuleSchema
  selects). `literal/1`, the atom clause of `resolve/2`, `elixir_module?/1`
  and the fallback of `line/1` were unreachable (every alias reaches the
  analyzer's resolved map; `line/1` only ever sees a call node) and are
  removed, so an unresolved name fails closed.
- run 3 (88d933f): `collect/3`'s definition clause and `verification/2`'s
  `{:mock, nil}` clause changed nothing observable and are removed;
  `mock_node/2`'s non-expect pipe is pinned by a new N5 row. Run 4 (8b30c5e):
  98 killed, 0 survived, no test lacking a red but the two S14 and S15 red.
- run 5 (c122f5a, after the verification fixes): 104 mutants, 100 killed, 4
  survived: `piped_verification/3`'s fallback clause (three mutants) and
  `verifier_call?/1` returning its input (an equivalent mutant: any name is
  truthy). The verifier shapes became `piped_verification/3`'s own clauses,
  with no boolean helper, and P6 pins the fallback (pipes into
  `post`/`json_response` and a bare name around an expectation).
- run 6 (370a580): 105 mutants, 105 killed, 0 survived. The tests it listed as
  lacking a red are S6 (e2e F1, F2), S14 and S15.
- The `rule_schema` and `config` records of those runs: run 2 left
  `RuleSchema.refuse_all_mocks/1` (narrow_clause) and `RuleSchema.validate/2`
  (with_passthrough) surviving, because a run of RuleSchema selects only
  `rule_schema_test.exs`; the `framework_test_mocks_manager mock_patterns` rows
  there killed both from run 3 on. `config` had no survivor in any run.

The final run on this branch, below, is the record of the code as it ships.

### The rebuilt branch's first full run (aec9fad)

`mix anchor.sabotage run --base 74ee077 --max all` on the rebuilt three code
commits: 261 mutants run, 208 killed, 44 survived, 7 invalid, 2 no_tests (the
`detect/1` of CaseOnBareArg and NoComparisonInIf: neither detector has a
mirror test), 203 skipped. What answered each survivor, before the run below:

- every `Anchor.Domain.Checks.*.detect/1` return_input (15, the two no_tests
  among them): a run selects only a detector's mirror test, which calls
  `detect_violations`; each detector now has a sibling
  `<check>_detector_test.exs` that calls `detect/1` on a violating snippet;
- `NoDependency.file_context/1` and `name_segments/1` (5): rows of
  `no_dependency_detector_test.exs` (an `Elixir.`-prefixed and a bare module
  name, several modules, another context, none);
- `StructGetterConvention.collect_alias/2` force_condition (2) and
  `put_as_alias/3` narrow_clause: `struct_getter_convention_alias_test.exs`
  (a misnamed getter through an `:as` alias; an `:as` alias of a non-literal
  name);
- `DependencyAnalyzer.module_scope/2` narrow_clause and `elixir_scope/1`
  force_condition: `dependency_analyzer_module_scope_test.exs` (the owner of
  an `Elixir.`-headed nested name, an attribute name, an Erlang atom);
- `Detector.report_only/1`, `run/3`, `some/3` narrow_clause: detector_test R5
  to R7 (a detector that reports whatever its rules);
- `Lint.detect/5` and `keyed/4` narrow_clause: `lint_detector_test.exs` (a
  file no rule selects is not detected; a key Lint cannot build is a crash on
  the file); `Lint.put_module_analyses/4` with_passthrough is equivalent and is
  on `.anchor/sabotage/survivors.txt` with its reason;
- `Cfa.Rename`: `expr/3`, `letrec/3`, `pattern/4` mutants: R12 (every binding
  form in one fixture, each binder renamed); `name_text/1` return_input (2):
  equivalent, the function was redundant and is removed; `receive_expr/3`
  (3): unreachable, the compiler lowers `receive` before Anchor reads Core,
  and the branch is removed.

### The rebuilt branch's second full run (bc32cf3)

254 mutants run, 240 killed, 7 survived (one held: `Lint.put_module_analyses/4`,
listed as equivalent), 7 invalid, 0 no_tests. The six new survivors:

- `Cfa.Rename.expr/3` (the `letrec` clause) and `pattern/4` (the `alias`
  clause) narrow_clause: equivalent, since a `letrec`'s and an alias
  pattern's subtrees are walked the same way by the generic step; both
  clauses are removed;
- `Cfa.Rename.pattern/4` `bitstr` and `map_pair` narrow_clause: R13 (a binary
  size reads the earlier segment's variable; a pinned map key is the outer
  binding, not one of its own);
- `StructGetterConvention.collect_alias/2` force_condition on the multi-alias
  clause: equivalent (add_plain_alias/2 adds nothing unless every part is an
  atom); on `.anchor/sabotage/survivors.txt` with that reason;
- `DependencyAnalyzer.module_scope/2` narrow_clause on the `__MODULE__`-headed
  clause: `dependency_analyzer_module_scope_test.exs` (such a name nests under
  the owner, a defimpl's module inside one).
